`forgot-password` / `reset-password` / `change-password`。Issue #3 交付清单中的 `login|logout` 为笔误，已更正。\n  - **D5–D14**：文案只取 `copy.ts`；成功后原地显示不跳转；密码不回显；多项字段错误一次返回并逐字段渲染；\n    不做需求外校验；邮箱归一化一致；不给密码字段加额外控件；登出后刷新/后退/直达受保护页都要求重新认证。\n\n## 交付物\n\n1. 注册页（已有，按 REQ-1-1-1 验收补断言）：字段/复选框/按钮名称、多项错误同显、保留 username+email、成功进登录页。\n2. 登录页（已有）：统一 `Invalid credentials`、cookie password is required`；成功后旧密码立即失效、新密码可登录。\n6. 后端：`POST /api/auth/forgot-password|reset-password|change-password`（`change-password` 需登录；错误体遵循 §3\n   `errors[]` 一次返回全部字段错误）+ Vitest 单测（校验规则、错误分支、会话失效、非泄露行为）。\n7. seed：`docs/seed-data.md` 账户表已由 PR #2 写入 seed 注册表（本任务仅核实，不改动）。\n\n## 共享契约（权威）\n\n`origin/develop` 的 `docs/architecture.md`（§3 API/错误/会话、§5         "type": "text",
        "text": "# M1 身份与访问（Issue #3）：注册/登录/找回密码/登出/改密码（REQ-1）\n\n- **Outcome:** REQ-1-1-1（注册）、REQ-1-1-2（登录）、REQ-1-1-3（找回密码）、REQ-1-2（登出）、REQ-1-3（改密码）\n  在交付应用（`frontend` 构建产物 + `backend` 单进程）上按需求文本逐字可用，并有后端 Vitest 单测与\n  Playwright 场景用例覆盖。基础 PR #2 已交付注册/登录/登出闭环；本任务替换 `/password-reset` 与\n  `/settings/*` 占位，补齐找回密码与改密码，并把 5 条需求纳入可重复的验收检查。\n- **Constraints:**\n  - 权威文本：`input/requirements.yaml` 的 REQ-1-1-1/1-1-2/1-1-3/1-2/1-3（引号内英文为精确 accessible name）。\n  - 共享契约：`docs/architecture.md`（§3 API/错误/会话、§5 数据模型、§6 seed、§7 UI、§9.10）、\n    `docs/product-plan.md` §3、`docs/acceptance-plan.md`、`docs/seed-data.md`。\n  - 文案一律来自 `frontend/src/contracts/copy.ts`（后端镜像 `backend/src/contracts/messages.js`，禁止散落字面量、\n    禁止自造需求未要求的错误文案）。\n  - 交付路径兼容：平台逐目录 `npm install` + `frontend npm run build` + `backend HOST/PORT npm run start`；\n    后端纯 ESM JS，无构建步骤；Node 20.19.3。\n  - 超出范围：真实邮件/验证码服务、邮箱验证页（需求明确排除）。\n- **Entry points:**\n  - 本 Issue：`local/run#3`（设计、决定、验收方案）。\n  - 实现 PR：`local/run#<PR>`（head `braid/issue-3-m1`，base `develop`）——实现计划、预演、实现与模块验收在此承接。\n  - 前置：PR #2（merge commit `c338578`，已合入 develop；脚手架、会话骨架、`copy.ts`、账户 seed 注册表）。\n- **Completion evidence:** 待实现 PR 回填（后端 Vitest 全绿；`pnpm typecheck` exit 0；`pnpm e2e`（含新增 M1 spec）全绿；\n  平台逐目录 install/build/start 路径检查；首轮结果与失败证据保留）。\n\n## 当前判断（2026-09-29，Issue #3 负责人）\n\n### 已可用（PR #2，无需重做，只需扩展与验收）\n- `POST /api/auth/register`：字段级全部错误一次返回（`errors[]`）、用户名/邮箱冲突文案、成功写\n  `email_verified=1`、不建会话。前端 `/register` 保留 username/email、清空密码字段，成功跳 `/login`。\n- `POST /api/auth/sign-in`（`identifier` = username 或 email）：所有失败一律 `Invalid credentials`；\n  成功建 cookie 会话。`POST /api/auth/sign-out`、`GET /api/auth/session`。\n- 账户菜单：触发按钮 accessible name `Account menu` → 链接 `Sign out` → 对话框 `Sign out` +\n  `Confirm sign out` / `Cancel`；`RequireAuth` 守卫受保护页。\n- 会话助手：`createSession` / `destroySession` / `revokeSessionsForUser(db, userId, {exceptSessionId})`。\n- 校验器：`validateUsername` / `validateEmail` / `validatePassword` / `validateConfirmation` / `validateTerms`\n  （`PASSWORD_REQUIRED` 可注入不同文案，供改密的 `Current password is required` 复用）。\n- 账户 seed（docs/seed-data.md 5 个账户）已在 seed 注册表内，启动默认执行、幂等不覆盖用户数据 → Issue 交付项 7 已满足。\n\n### 待实现（本任务范围）\n1. `POST /api/auth/forgot-password`、`POST /api/auth/reset-password`（REQ-1-1-3）。\n2. `POST /api/auth/change-password`（REQ-1-3，需 `requireAuth`）。\n3. 前端 `/password-reset` 两步恢复流程（替换占位页）。\n4. 前端 `/settings/password` → “Password and authentication” 安全表单（替换占位页；`/settings/*` 改为嵌套路由）。\n5. 后端 Vitest：新端点校验规则、错误分支、会话失效、非泄露行为。\n6. Playwright：5 条需求对应用例（含刷新持久化、密码不回显、已知/未知邮箱一致）。\n\n## 设计（供 PR 负责人实施／复核）\n\n### API（全部 `POST /api/auth/*`，错误体遵循 §3：`errors[]` 一次返回全部字段错误）\n\n| 端点 | 认证 | 请求体 | 成功 | 字段错误（`field` → 文案） |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `register`（已有） | 无 | `username,email,password,confirmPassword,agreeToTerms` | 201 `{user}` | `username`→`Username is required`/`Username format is invalid`/`Username already exists`；`email`→`Email is required`/`Email format is invalid`/`Email already exists`；`password`→`Password is required`/`Password requirements are not satisfied`；`confirmPassword`→`Confirm password is required`/`Password confirmation does not match`；`agreeToTerms`→`Agree to terms is required` |\n| `sign-in`（已有） | 无 | `identifier,password` | 200 `{user}` + cookie | 无字段错误：一律 `Invalid credentials`（含字段缺失） |\n| `sign-out`（已有） | cookie 可选 | — | 200 `{ok}` + 清 cookie | — |\n| `forgot-password` | 无 | `email` | 200 `{ok:true}`（对已注册与未注册邮箱响应完全相同） | `email`→`Email is required`/`Email format is invalid` |\n| `reset-password` | 无 | `email,code,newPassword,confirmPassword` | 200 `{ok:true, message:'Password updated'}` | `code`→`Verification code is invalid`；`newPassword`→`Password is required`/`Password requirements are not satisfied`；`confirmPassword`→`Confirm password is required`/`Password confirmation does not match`；未注册邮箱→按 `code` 字段的非泄露处理（见决定 D2） |\n| `change-password` | `requireAuth` | `currentPassword,newPassword,confirmPassword` | 200 `{ok:true, message:'Password updated'}` | `currentPassword`→`Current password is required`（空）/`Current password is incorrect`（错误）；`newPassword`→`Password is required`/`Password requirements are not satisfied`；`confirmPassword`→`Confirm password is required`/`Password confirmation does not match`；未登录→401 `Authentication required` |\n\n- 未在需求中出现的字段错误文案不得自造（例如不为“未注册邮箱”引入新文案）。\n- 任何响应不得包含 `password_hash`；密码字段不回显（前端提交失败后清空、且不回填）。\n- `reset-password` / `change-password` 成功后在**同一事务**内更新 `password_hash` 并调用\n  `revokeSessionsForUser()`（语义见 D1 / D1b）。`reset-password` 无服务端待处理状态（不需要 recovery 表）：\n  “恢复上下文”= 提交时的 `email` + `code`，服务端重新校验。`forgot-password` 只做邮箱格式校验，\n  不查询、不泄露账户是否存在。\n\n### 前端\n\n- `/password-reset`（由登录页链接 `Forgot password` 打开；步骤由 URL 驱动，见 D3）\n  - 第 1 步：`Email` 字段 + `Send reset link` 按钮（+ 返回 `Sign in` 链接）。\n  - 第 2 步（`?step=2&email=…`）：固定验证码文本 `123456` 作为**独立可见元素**（绝不嵌入其它句子，\n    保证按精确文本可断言），`Verification code` / `New password` / `Confirm password` 字段 + `Reset password` 按钮。\n  - 失败：按 `errors[]` 在对应字段旁逐字显示；成功后（`?step=done`）显示 `Password updated`（不跳转、不产生邮件/链接）。\n  - 密码字段提交失败后清空、不回显。\n- `/settings/*`：改为嵌套路由。`Password and authentication` 链接进入 `/settings/password`，渲染安全表单\n  （`Current password` / `New password` / `Confirm password` + `Update password`），成功后显示 `Password updated`，\n  失败按字段显示错误；空 current 显示 `Current password is required`。\n- 其余页面（注册/登录/账户菜单/登出确认）保持 PR #2 行为，仅按验收补断言。\n\n## 决定（Issue #3）\n\n经过原生 advisor 咨询（2026-09-29，见“依据”），确定如下。**关键事实：需求原文从不规定 API 路径**，全部断言都是页面级的\n（accessible name、可见文案、登录/登出行为），因此 API 形状按已发布契约与最小改动决定。\n\n- **D1 改密后的会话语义：保留调用者当前会话，只吊销该用户的其它会话**\n  （`revokeSessionsForUser(db, user.id, { exceptSessionId: req.sessionId })`）。理由：REQ-1-3 场景 1 的流程是\n  “改密成功 → 页面显示 `Password updated` → 从账户菜单 `Sign out` → 用新密码登录”，把操作者登出会使该流程与断言失效；\n  架构 §3 的“改密即时失效会话”按“其它会话不得继续有效”理解。\n- **D1b 找回密码成功：吊销该用户全部会话**（`revokeSessionsForUser` 不带 except，恢复流程是未认证的，没有当前会话要保），\n  **不自动登录、不建新会话**；成功后只显示 `Password updated`，后续用新密码走正常登录。\n- **D2 未知邮箱在恢复第 2 步：与错误验证码完全相同的表现** —— `Verification code is invalid`（字段 `code`）。\n  理由：不泄露句是双重表述（description 专句 + 场景 1 THEN「do not indicate whether the email exists」），而需求只为\n  “错误验证码”规定了确切文案，未给未知邮箱任何文案，隐藏验收无法断言不存在的字符串。无论哪条路径都**不得修改任何账户**。\n- **D3 恢复流程步骤由 URL 驱动**：`/password-reset`（第 1 步）→ `/password-reset?step=2&email=…`（第 2 步，显示独立文本\n  `123456` 与重置表单）→ 成功后 `/password-reset?step=done`（显示 `Password updated`）。\n  - `123456` 来自 `copy.ts` 的 `VERIFICATION_CODE`，**不在 mount 时发任何请求**：刷新/重新打开只重渲染，\n    天然满足“刷新不产生邮件/链接/第二个账户”，并覆盖“刷新后结果仍可见”的通用 THEN。\n  - 第 1 步 `Send reset link` 调 `POST /api/auth/forgot-password` 仅做邮箱格式校验（已注册/未注册邮箱响应完全相同），\n    成功后才跳转；`?step=2` 缺少 `email` 时回落第 1 步。直接访问 `?step=2` 允许（验证码是本地固定值，不泄露存在性）。\n- **D4 接口命名：保留已发布的 `sign-in`/`sign-out` 为 canonical，不添加 `login`/`logout` 别名**；\n  Issue #3 交付清单里的 `login|logout` 按笔误更正（需求原文未规定路径，基础契约、前端 client、单测与 e2e 均已使用 sign-in/sign-out；\n  加别名只会引入无消费者的第二契约面）。新端点用既有风格：`/api/auth/forgot-password`、`/api/auth/reset-password`、\n  `/api/auth/change-password`。\n- **D5 文案**：只使用 `copy.ts` 已定义的常量；需求未给出的错误文案不自造。找回成功/改密成功都显示 `Password updated`。\n- **D6 seed 账户保护**：Playwright 用例**不得修改 seed 账户密码**（seed 为 `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`，\n  改动会在同一次 e2e 运行的共享临时库中残留并影响其它用例）。改密/找回用例一律先注册唯一 `pw-*` 新账户。\n- **D7 多项错误**：新端点在一次提交内返回全部字段错误（与 §3 一致），前端逐字段渲染；\n  例如改密“current 错 + 确认不匹配”两条同时显示。\n- **D8 成功后原地显示，不自动跳转**：`Password updated` 必须在当前页显示（需求用的是 “directly displays”），\n  自动 navigate 会造成断言竞态。\n- **D9 密码不回显**：注册/找回/改密提交失败后清空所有密码字段（保留 username/email/identifier 等非敏感输入），\n  成功后也清空；任何响应不含 `password_hash`。\n- **D10 `123456` 是独立可见文本节点**（需求：“not only embedded inside a longer instruction”）：\n  单独元素渲染，任何说明句中都不得再出现这串数字。\n- **D11 不做需求外的校验**：例如“新密码必须不同于旧密码”——需求未规定，不得添加。\n- **D12 邮箱归一化一致**：注册/登录/找回/重置一律复用 `validateEmail`（trim + 小写）；\n  密码不做 trim/大小写处理、逐字符比较。\n- **D13 不给密码字段加可见性切换等额外控件**（需求写 “exactly one” 的字段/按钮，额外同名控件会破坏唯一性断言）。\n- **D14 登出后的不可恢复链**：`signOut()` 清空客户端用户状态；`RequireAuth` 以服务端 `GET /api/auth/session` 为准，\n  保证刷新/后退/直达受保护页都回到未认证态并显示 `Sign in`。\n\n## 验收方案（模块级，docs/acceptance-plan.md 分层）\n\nPlaywright（`e2e/identity.spec.ts`，新）：每条需求一组用例，`getByRole(role,{name})` 精确名称、文案逐字断言、\n每条用例新 context 起始（fresh unauthenticated）、对象名带唯一后缀、断言用唯一 token 限定范围。\n\n高风险点（advisor 咨询后列出的自有断言，务必覆盖）：引号名逐条唯一存在（strict mode）——`Create an account` 链接、\n`Account menu` 按钮、`Sign out` 链接（必须是 link role，不能是 menuitem）、各字段 textbox；\n`123456` 按精确文本命中；成功后不跳转；失败后密码输入为空、username/email 保留；多项错误同时显示；\n登出后刷新/后退/直达受保护页都要重新认证；每类失败都补“旧密码仍可登录 / 候选新密码不可登录”的端到端断言；\n恢复第 2 步刷新时不得发出写请求（用 `page.on('request')` 断言无 `POST /api/auth/*`）且 `123456` 仍可见。\n\n- REQ-1-1-1：注册成功 → 进入登录页、邮箱可登录、账户菜单显示用户名、刷新仍在；\n  “-bad / not-an-email / short / different / 未勾选”一次提交显示 username/email/password/confirm/terms 五条文案并保留\n  username+email、密码输入为空、URL 仍在 `/register`；重复用户名显示 `Username already exists` 且保留两个输入；\n  失败提交后该凭据无法登录（`Invalid credentials`）→ 未创建账户。\n- REQ-1-1-2：用 username 与 email 分别登录成功；未知账户/错误密码都只显示 `Invalid credentials` 且无账户菜单；\n  登录后刷新受保护页仍显示用户名；未登录直达受保护页要求认证。\n- REQ-1-1-3：新注册账户走恢复流程（已知邮箱）：第 2 步显示独立文本 `123456`；错误码 `000000` →\n  `Verification code is invalid`、旧密码仍可登录、候选新密码不可登录；正确码 + 合规新密码 → `Password updated`，\n  之后旧密码登录失败、新密码成功。未知邮箱：同样进入第 2 步、同样显示 `123456`、不泄露存在性；\n  刷新恢复页不产生邮件/链接/新账户。密码不回显。\n- REQ-1-2：确认登出后未认证（刷新、浏览器后退、直达受保护页都要求重新认证并显示 `Sign in`）；\n  `Cancel` 或关闭对话框保留会话与原页面。\n- REQ-1-3：成功流（改密 → `Password updated` → 登出 → 新密码可登录、旧密码失败）；空 current →\n  `Current password is required`；错误 current → `Current password is incorrect`；不合规新密码 →\n  `Password requirements are not satisfied`；确认不一致 → `Password confirmation does not match`；\n  失败后旧密码仍可登录；新密码不回显。\n\n后端 Vitest（`backend/test/`，扩展 `auth.test.js` 或新增 `identity-recovery.test.js`）：\n每个新端点的成功/字段错误/多项错误一次返回、非泄露（已注册与未注册邮箱响应逐字节一致）、\n重置后旧会话失效（同用户两个会话都失效）、改密后旧密码不能登录而新密码可以、响应不含 `password_hash`。\n\n整合验收（不在本任务）：develop → main 整合 PR 由根负责人执行全量 `pnpm e2e` 与平台启动路径冒烟。\n\n## 依据（原生 advisor 咨询，2026-09-29）\n\n- 需求原文不规定任何 API 路径 → 命名以已发布契约为准（D4）。\n- D1 的反例分析：登出操作者会使 REQ-1-3 场景 1 的“改密成功后从账户菜单登出”流程与 `Account menu` 断言直接失败；\n  且 `revokeSessionsForUser()` 自带的 `exceptSessionId` 参数本身就是为基础 PR 预留的“保留当前会话”用法。\n- D2 的强度比较：不泄露句有 description 专句 + 场景 THEN 双重表述；需求只为错误验证码给出确切文案。\n- D3 的取舍：URL 状态同时覆盖“刷新不产生副作用”与“刷新后 `123456`/成功文案仍可见”两种可能断言；\n  代价是直接访问 `?step=2` 也能看到表单（需求未禁止，且验证码是本地演示值）。\n\n## 未决问题 / 风险\n\n- **待根 Issue #1 确认（不阻塞实现）**：D4 对交付清单端点名的更正；以及是否把 D1/D1b 的会话语义、D2 的非泄露处理\n  作为澄清写进 `docs/architecture.md` §3（或 §9 追加条目）。若确认，由 PR #3 一并更新文档；未确认前以本 packet 为准。\n- 潜在过度断言（概率低，已对冲）：隐藏验收若断言“正确码 + 未知邮箱时不得出现 `Verification code is invalid`”（D2），\n  或“改密后当前浏览器也被登出”（D1）、或“恢复第 2 步刷新后回到 Email 步”（D3），则需按证据重新裁决——三者都与需求原文的\n  其他句子冲突，届时先在本 Issue 讨论。\n- 参考图缺口：`github-account-menu-sign-out.png` 实际内容是登录后 Dashboard（无账户菜单/登出对话框图像证据）；\n  两张找回密码图都没有验证码 UI；`github-password-settings.png` 是 “Sign in methods” 列表而非改密表单。\n  因此登出确认 UI、验证码展示、改密表单的形态**只按需求文本实现**，截图仅作风格参考（单列居中、宽主按钮、字段标签）。\n  该观察已补充到 `docs/ui-reference-notes.md` 的图像缺口。\n\n## Supporting material\n\n- {};\n    const username = validateUsername(body.username);\n    const email = validateEmail(body.email);\n    const password = validatePassword(body.password);\n    const confirmation = validateConfirmation(body.confirmPassword ?? {};\n    const identifier = String(body.identifier ?? body.usernameOrEmail ?? body.username ?? body.email ?? '' : String(raw);\n}\n\n/** Username: 1–39 lowercase letters/digits/single hyphens, no leading/trailing hyphen. */\nexport function validateUsername(raw, field = 'username') {\n  const value = text(raw).trim();\n  if (!value) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'username_required', message: messages.USERNAME_REQUIRED, field };\n  }\n  if (value.length > 39 || !USERNAME_RE.test(value)) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'username_format_invalid', message: messages.USERNAME_FORMAT_INVALID, field };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/**\n * Email: trimmed, exactly one `@`, at most 254 characters, and at least one dot\n * with non-empty domain labels after the `@`.\n */\nexport function validateEmail(raw, field = 'email') {\n  const value = text(raw).trim().toLowerCase();\n 'email_format_invalid', message: messages.EMAIL_FORMAT_INVALID, field });\n  if (value.length > 254 || parts.length !== 2) return invalid();\n  const [local, domain] = parts;\n  if (/\\s/.test(domain)) return invalid();\n  return { ok: true, value, field };\n}\n\n/**\n * Password: 12–128 characters with at least one uppercase letter, one lowercase\n * letter, one digit and one non-alphanumeric character, and no whitespace.\n */\nexport function validatePassword(raw, { field = 'password', requiredMessage = messages.PASSWORD_REQUIRED } = {}) {\n  const value = text(raw);\n  if (!value) {\n    return { ok: false, code: 'password_required', message: requiredMessage, field };\n  }\n  const invalid = () => ({\n    ok: false,\n    code: 'password_requirements_not_satisfied',\n    message: messages.PASSWORD_REQUIREMENTS,\n    field,\n  });\n  if (value.length < 12 || value.length > 128) return invalid();\n  if (/\\s/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[A-Z]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[a-z]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[0-9]/.test(value)) return invalid();\n  if (!/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(value)) Sign-out, password change and password recovery must make the change\n * visible to subsequent requests immediately — that is what\n * `revokeSessionsForUser()` is for (M1 calls it on password change/recovery).\n *\n * The cookie is httpOnly + SameSite=Lax and deliberately not `Secure`: the\n * delivered application is served over plain HTTP by the platform start command.\n */\nimport crypto from 'node:crypto';\nimport { config } from '../config.js';\n\n/** Public shape of a user (never includes password_hash). */\nexport function publicUser(row) {\n  if (!row) return null;\n  return {\n    id: row.id,\n    username: row.username,\n    email: row.email,\n    emailVerified: Boolean(row.email_verified),\n    createdAt: row.created_at,\n  };\n}\n\nexport function createSession(db, userId) {\n  const id = crypto.randomBytes(24).toString('hex');\n  db.prepare('INSERT INTO sessions (id, user_id) VALUES (?, ?)').run(id, userId);\n  return id;\n}\n\nexport function getSessionUser(db, sessionId) {\n  if (!sessionId) return null;\n  const row = db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT u.* FROM sessions s JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id WHERE s.id = ?`,\n    )\n    .get(sessionId);\n  return row || null;\n}\n\nexport function destroySession(db, sessionId) {\n  if (!sessionId) return false;\n  return db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE id = ?').run(sessionId).changes > 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Invalidate every session of a user (used after a password change / recovery so\n * the old login state stops working immediately).\n * @param {{exceptSessionId?: string|null}} [options] keep the caller's own session alive\n */\nexport function revokeSessionsForUser(db, userId, options = {}) {\n  const { exceptSessionId = null } = options;\n  if (exceptSessionId) {\n    return db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ? AND id <> ?').run(userId, exceptSessionId).changes;\n  }\n  return db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE user_id = ?').run(userId).changes;\n}\n\nexport function setSessionCookie(res, sessionId) {\n  res.cookie(config.sessionCookieName, sessionId, {\n    httpOnly: true,\n    sameSite: 'lax',\n    path: '/',\n    maxAge: config.sessionTtlMs,\n  });\n}\n\nexport function clearSessionCookie(res) {\n  res.clearCookie(config.sessionCookieName, {\n    httpOnly: true,\n    sameSite: 'lax',\n    path: '/',\n  });\n}\n\nexport function readSessionId(req) {\n  return req.cookies?.[config.sessionCookieName] || null;\n}\n"

this.code = code;\n    this.field = extra.field;\n    this.errors = extra.errors;\n  }\n\n  toBody() {\n    const primary = { code: this.code, message: this.message };\n    if (this.field) primary.field = this.field;\n    const body = { error: primary };\n    // `errors[]` lists every field problem of one submission (docs/architecture.md §3).\n    // It is emitted whenever field WHERE id = ?').run(hashPassword(newPassword.value), user.id);\n      // Recovery is unauthenticated: every session of the user stops working (D1b).\n      revokeSessionsForUser(db, user.id);\n    });\n    res.json({ ok: true, message: messages.PASSWORD_UPDATED });\n  });\n\n  // REQ-1-3: authenticated password change.           "content": "import { useState } from 'react';\nimport type { FormEvent } from 'react';\nimport { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';\nimport { api, ApiError } from '../../api/client';\nimport { ErrorAlert, SuccessAlert } from '../../components/Alerts';\nimport { Button } from '../../components/Button';\nimport { TextField } from '../../components/TextField';\nimport { auth as authCopy, errors as errorCopy, VERIFICATION_CODE } from '../../contracts/copy';\n\n/**\n * Account recovery (REQ-1-1-3).\n *\n * The step state lives in the URL (docs decision D3):\n *   /password-reset                    — step 1: \"Email\" + \"Send reset link\"\n *   /password-reset?step=2&email=…     — step 2: fixed code \"123456\" + reset form\n *   /password-reset?step=done          — success: \"Password updated\"\n *\n * Mounting any step sends no request, so refreshing never produces an email, a\n * link or a second account, and the visible result survives a reload.\n */\nexport function PasswordResetPage() {\n  const [params] = useSearchParams();\n  const step = params.get('step');\n  const email = (params.get('email') ?? '').trim();\n\n  if (step === 'done') return <ResetDoneStep />;\n  if (step === '2' && email) return <ResetStep email={email.toLowerCase()} />;\n  return <RequestStep />;\n}\n\n/** Step 1: ask for the email address. Registered and unknown emails behave the same. */\nfunction RequestStep() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');\n  const [error, setError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n\n  async function onSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {\n    event.preventDefault();\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      // Only the email format is validated server-side; both outcomes advance.\n      await api.post('/api/auth/forgot-password', { email });\n      navigate(`/password-reset?step=2&email=${encodeURIComponent(email.trim().toLowerCase())}`);\n    } catch (caught) {\n      setError(caught instanceof ApiError ? caught : new ApiError(0, { code: 'unknown', message: errorCopy.unknown }));\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-sm mt-10\">\n      <h1 className=\"page-title text-center\">{authCopy.forgotPassword}</h1>\n\n      <div className=\"app-card p-4 mt-4\">\n        {error ? <ErrorAlertList errors={error} /> : null}\n        <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate>\n          <TextField\n            label={authCopy.email}\n            name=\"email\"\n            type=\"email\"\n            autoComplete=\"email\"\n            value={email}\n            error={error?.fieldError('email')}\n            onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}\n          />\n          <Button type=\"submit\" variant=\"primary\" className=\"w-full\" disabled={submitting}>\n            {authCopy.sendResetLink}\n          </Button>\n        </form>\n\n        <p className=\"mt-4 text-sm m-0\">\n          <Link className=\"text-accent\" to=\"/login\">\n            {authCopy.signIn}\n          </Link>\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Step 2: the fixed local demonstration code is shown as its own visible text\n * value (never embedded in a longer sentence), above the reset form.\n */\nfunction ResetStep({ email }: { email: string }) {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [code, setCode] = useState('');\n  const [values, setValues] = useState({ newPassword: '', confirmPassword: '' });\n  const [error, setError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n\n  const update = (field: 'newPassword' | 'confirmPassword', value: string) =>\n    setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: value }));\n\n  async function onSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {\n    event.preventDefault();\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      await api.post('/api/auth/reset-password', {\n        email,\n        code,\n        newPassword: values.newPassword,\n        confirmPassword: values.confirmPassword,\n      });\n      navigate('/password-reset?step=done');\n    } catch (caught) {\n      const apiError =\n        caught instanceof ApiError ? caught : new ApiError(0, { code: 'unknown', message: errorCopy.unknown });\n      setError(apiError);\n      // Submitted password values are never redisplayed (D9); the code keeps.\n      setValues({ newPassword: '', confirmPassword: '' });\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-sm mt-10\">\n      <h1 className=\"page-title text-center\">{authCopy.forgotPassword}</h1>\n\n      <div className=\"app-card p-4 mt-4\">\n        <p className=\"text-sm text-muted m-0\">Local demonstration verification code:</p>\n        <p className=\"verification-code m-0 mt-1\">{VERIFICATION_CODE}</p>\n\n        {error ? (\n          <div className=\"mt-3\">\n            <ErrorAlertList errors={error} />\n          </div>\n        ) : null}\n\n        <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate className=\"mt-2\">\n          <TextField\n            label={authCopy.verificationCode}\n            name=\"code\"\n            autoComplete=\"one-time-code\"\n            value={code}\n            error={error?.fieldError('code')}\n            onChange={(event) => setCode(event.target.value)}\n          />\n          <TextField\n            label={authCopy.newPassword}\n            name=\"newPassword\"\n            type=\"password\"\n            autoComplete=\"new-password\"\n            value={values.newPassword}\n            error={error?.fieldError('newPassword')}\n            onChange={(event) => update('newPassword', event.target.value)}\n          />\n          <TextField\n            label={authCopy.confirmPassword}\n            name=\"confirmPassword\"\n            type=\"password\"\n            autoComplete=\"new-password\"\n            value={values.confirmPassword}\n            error={error?.fieldError('confirmPassword')}\n            onChange={(event) => update('confirmPassword', event.target.value)}\n          />\n          <Button type=\"submit\" variant=\"primary\" className=\"w-full\" disabled={submitting}>\n            {authCopy.resetPassword}\n          </Button>\n        </form>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/** Success step: the message is displayed in place; nothing else happens. */\nfunction ResetDoneStep() {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-sm mt-10\">\n      <h1 className=\"page-title text-center\">{authCopy.forgotPassword}</h1>\n\n      <div className=\"app-card p-4 mt-4\">\n        <SuccessAlert>{errorCopy.passwordUpdated}</SuccessAlert>\n        <p className=\"text-sm m-0\">\n          <Link className=\"text-accent\" to=\"/login\">\n            {authCopy.signIn}\n          </Link>\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n/** Field problems rendered next to their field; non-field ones above the form. */\nfunction ErrorAlertList({ errors }: { errors: ApiError }) {\n  const general = errors.generalErrors();\n  return (\n    <>\n      {general.map((message) => (\n        <ErrorAlert key={message}>{message}</ErrorAlert>\n      ))}\n    </>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default PasswordResetPage;\n"

<SuccessAlert>{errorCopy.passwordUpdated}</SuccessAlert> : null}\n\n        <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate>\n          <TextField\n            label={authCopy.currentPassword}\n            name=\"currentPassword\"\n            type=\"password\"\n            autoComplete=\"current-password\"\n            value={values.currentPassword}\n            error={error?.fieldError('currentPassword')}\n            onChange={(event) => update('currentPassword', event.target.value)}\n          />\n          <TextField\n            label={authCopy.newPassword}\n            name=\"newPassword\"\n            type=\"password\"\n            autoComplete=\"new-password\"\n            help=\"12–128 characters with upper case, lower case, a digit and a special character.\"\n            value={values.newPassword}\n            error={error?.fieldError('newPassword')}\n            onChange={(event) => update('newPassword', event.target.value)}\n          />\n          <TextField\n            label={authCopy.confirmPassword}\n            name=\"confirmPassword\"\n            type=\"password\"\n            autoComplete=\"new-password\"\n            value={values.confirmPassword}\n            error={error?.fieldError('confirmPassword')}\n            onChange={(event) => update('confirmPassword', event.target.value)}\n          />\n          <Button type=\"submit\" variant=\"primary\" disabled={submitting}>\n            {authCopy.updatePassword}\n          </Button>\n        </form>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default PasswordSettingsPage;\n",

cannot sign in.\n    await signIn(page, account.email, account.password);\n    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: authCopy.accountMenu })).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(page.getByText(account.username)).toBeVisible();\n\n    // The session (and the visible username) survives a reload.\n    await page.reload();\n    await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: authCopy.accountMenu })).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(page.getByText(account.username)).toBeVisible();\n  });\n\n  test('empty required fields are all reported and no account is created', async ({      errorCopy.emailRequired,\n      errorCopy.passwordRequired,\n      errorCopy.passwordConfirmationRequired,\n      errorCopy.termsRequired,\n    ]) {\n      await expect(page.getByText(message, { });\n});\n\ntest.describe('REQ-1-1-3 password recovery', () => {\n  test('wrong code keeps the old password working and shows the exact message', async ({ page }) => {\n    const account = await
